# Blissymbol dictionary

4675 headwords, built from `blissdata_words.js` and `blissdata_chars.js` (1150 symbols). Generated by `tools/build_dictionary.py`; do not edit by hand.

Each entry gives the English headword, any other English names for it (*also called*), and its composition: the words or symbols it is built from, joined with `+`, in the order they are written from left to right. Symbols marked as indicators (e.g. *indicator (description)*) are placed above or after the symbol they modify.

Full symbol list with sizes, usage counts and file links: [`symbols_index.csv`](symbols_index.csv). Symbol drawings: [`../svg/`](../svg/), 3D models: [`../3d/`](../3d/), browsable gallery: [`../gallery/index.html`](../gallery/index.html).
